- **self-compact**: Keep Copilot CLI working context lean while preserving decisions, active state, drafts, and session-bound resources through deliberate compaction. Use when a task finishes, work changes phase, a review round ends, tool history grows, the agent becomes stuck or repetitive, a durable work order enables a soft reset, or a long-lived session needs retirement. ([dfrysinger/skills](https://github.com/dfrysinger/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/self-compact) · ★3)
- **self-compact**: Shrink this session's context and keep the work running. A session cannot run the /compact client command itself, so it messages itself through the zcode-sessions MCP — a delivered `/compact &lt;handoff&gt;` performs the compaction, a second wake message resumes the work. Triggers — `/self-compact`, «закомпакть сессию», «ужми контекст сессии и продолжай работу», «контекст раздулся — сократи его, не останавливаясь». Use when the user asks to compact a long-running session's context without ending the work. ([morr/dotfiles](https://github.com/morr/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/zcode-skills/self-compact) · ★1)

- **self-consistency**: Generate N independent reasoning paths and vote on the answer. Use for architectural trade-offs, ambiguous design decisions, or when single-path reasoning risks locking onto the first plausible answer. Paper: Wang et al. 2022. ([nguyenthienthanh/archon](https://github.com/nguyenthienthanh/archon/tree/HEAD/archon-os/skills/self-consistency) · ★26 · 2 copies)
- **self-consistency**: Sample N independent reasoning chains for the same question and majority-vote the final answer. Trades cost for accuracy — robust on math and logic, surprisingly cheap with smaller-than-frontier models. ([SamoTech/skills-tree](https://github.com/SamoTech/skills-tree/tree/HEAD/agent-skills/self-consistency) · ★4)
- **self-consistency**: Run multiple independent reasoning passes and find consensus. ([Thomashighbaugh/opencode](https://github.com/Thomashighbaugh/opencode/tree/HEAD/skills/self-consistency) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **self-consistency**: Don't trust one chain of reasoning on a hard problem. Solve it several ways, then believe only what the independent attempts agree on. ([opelpleple/meta-skills](https://github.com/opelpleple/meta-skills/tree/HEAD/self-consistency))
- **self-consistency**: Sample multiple independent traces and take consensus. USE FOR verifiable answers. DO NOT USE for open-ended creative work. - **self-consistency**: For a hard reasoning step or a high-stakes decision, sample several independent solutions and take the consistent answer instead of trusting one shot. Use on tricky bugs, ambiguous design calls, math/logic, or any answer where being wrong is expensive. Trigger with /self-consistency or "sample multiple", "are you sure", "vote on this", "think this through N ways". - **self-consistency-check**: Force the combination step that a weaker model can't do in one pass — surface conflicts between constraints, and interactions between requirements and edge cases, using a pairwise sweep, a fresh-context cold read by a subagent that never saw your reasoning, and N-version divergence on the hard kernel. Trigger this after producing a plan, design, spec, or multi-constraint solution and BEFORE committing to it — especially when the task carries more than ~3 interacting constraints, a schema/interface decision, or high stakes. Do NOT trigger for a single-constraint or trivial task (17.3 tiers say… ([UnpaidAttention/fable5-methodology](https://github.com/UnpaidAttention/fable5-methodology/tree/HEAD/skills/self-consistency-check) · ★94)

- **self-consistency-reasoning**: Use when the user invokes /self-consistency-reasoning or wants to apply self-consistency sampling (Wang et al., ICLR 2023) as a meta-layer over any Vidbyte reasoning strategy. Samples N=5 diverse reasoning paths via temperature decoding from a single base strategy, then majority-votes the most consistent answer. Pairs with any CoT-prompted strategy in the Vidbyte catalog G�� no additional training required. ([cerredz/Vidbyte-Skills](https://github.com/cerredz/Vidbyte-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/self-consistency-reasoning))
- **self-consistency-trace**: Use when the user invokes /self-consistency-trace or asks for a standalone reasoning trace using Self-Consistency (Wang et al., ICLR 2023). Samples N=5 diverse reasoning paths from a base strategy via temperature decoding, then majority-votes the most consistent answer. Produces a durable audit trail in memory/{question_name}.md showing all five paths and the vote tally. Use this when a single reasoning path feels unreliable and you want consensus across diverse reasoning chains to surface the most robust answer. - **self-evolution-stopping-rule**: Decide online when to stop a self-evolving loop (prompt, skill, harness or program evolution) and which earlier artifact to return. Replaces fixed round budgets; the returned artifact still goes through the acceptance gate. Derived from "When Is Enough Enough in Self-Evolving LLM Systems?" (arXiv:2610.04756).
